<ruby><rb>然而</rb><rt>Jün-r</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>祭物</rb><rt>tsi-veh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>特爲</rb><rt>*deh-we*</rt><rb>年年</rb><rt>nyin-nyin</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>提醒</rb><rt>di-sing</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>*gyi-go*</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>雄牛</rb><rt>yüong-ngeo</rt><rb>雄山羊個</rb><rt>yüong-sæn-yiang-go</rt><rb>血</rb><rt>hyüih</rt><rb>斷</rb><rt>tön</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>除掉</rb><rt>djü-diao</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] *ʽÔh* *wô*, pih-ding we hyih-tsôh. 或話，必定會歇作。（原註）</sup>

<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>Keh-lah</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>*Kyi-toh*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>世間上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>祭祀</rb><rt>Tsi-z</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>福禮</rb><rt>foh-li</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>要個</rb><rt>iao-go</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>備好</rb><rt>be-hao</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>肌身</rb><rt>kyi-sing</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>燒化個</rb><rt>siao-hwô-go</rt><rb>祭物</rb><rt>tsi-veh</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>贖罪個</rb><rt>joh-ze-go</rt><rb>祭物</rb><rt>*tsi-veh*</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>歡喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，『<ruby><rb>且看</rb><rt>Tsʽia-kʽen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>來東</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>做成</rb><rt>tso-dzing</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>旨意</rb><rt>ts-i</rt></ruby>。』<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>*ziang*</rt><rb>經卷裏</rb><rt>kying-kyün-li</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>*sô*</rt><rb>寫的</rb><rt>sia-tih</rt><rb>指點</rb><rt>ts-tin</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。」 <sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>上文</rb><rt>Zông-veng</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>祭祀</rb><rt>Tsi-z</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>福禮</rb><rt>foh-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>燒化個</rb><rt>siao-hwô-go</rt><rb>祭物</rb><rt>tsi-veh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>贖罪個</rb><rt>joh-ze-go</rt><rb>祭物</rb><rt>*tsi-veh*</rt></ruby>，」（<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>獻上個</rb><rt>hyin-zông-go</rt></ruby>，）「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>歡喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt></ruby>。」<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>且看</rb><rt>Tsʽia-kʽen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>來東</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>做成</rb><rt>tso-dzing</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>旨意</rb><rt>ts-i</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>*Keh*</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>*z*</rt><rb>除掉</rb><rt>djü-diao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>前頭</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>設立</rb><rt>shih-lih</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>We-leh</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>旨意</rb><rt>ts-i</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>靠着</rb><rt>kʽao-djôh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u> <ruby><rb><u>基督</u>個</rb><rt>Kyi-toh-go</rt><rb>肌身</rb><rt>kyi-sing</rt><rb>只消</rb><rt>tsih-siao</rt><rb>一遭</rb><rt>ih-tsao</rt><rb>獻上</rb><rt>hyin-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>尊起了個</rb><rt>tseng-kyʽi-liao-go</rt></ruby>。

<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>Væn-pah</rt><rb>祭司</rb><rt>tsi-s</rt><rb>每日</rb><rt>me-nyih</rt><rb>立仔</rb><rt>lih-ts</rt><rb>供奉</rb><rt>kong-vong</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>*Jing-ming*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>屢次</rb><rt>le-tsʽ</rt><rb>獻上</rb><rt>hyin-zông</rt><rb>做樣</rb><rt>tso-yiang</rt><rb>祭物</rb><rt>tsi-veh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>脫掉</rb><rt>tʽeh-diao</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt><rb>箇位</rb><rt>keh-we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>*Kyi-toh*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>獻上</rb><rt>hyin-zông</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>贖罪</rb><rt>joh-ze</rt><rb>祭物</rb><rt>tsi-veh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>長通</rb><rt>dziang-tʽong</rt><rb>坐</rb><rt>zo</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>順手邊</rb><rt>jing-siu-pin</rt></ruby>；<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>以後</rb><rt>yi-ʽeo</rt><rb>單等</rb><rt>tæn-teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>仇敵</rb><rt>dziu-dih</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>踏腳凳</rb><rt>dah-kyiah-teng</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>獻上</rb><rt>hyin-zông</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>祭物</rb><rt>*tsi-veh*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>尊起個</rb><rt>tseng-kyʽi-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>永遠</rb><rt>üong-yün</rt><rb>全備</rb><rt>djün-be</rt></ruby>。

<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>箇位</rb><rt>Keh-we</rt><rb>聖靈</rb><rt>Sing-Ling</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>對證</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>預先</rb><rt>yü-sin</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>，<sup>16</sup> 「<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>CÜ</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，『<ruby><rb>過了</rb><rt>Ko-leh</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>立個</rb><rt>lih-go</rt><rb>約</rb><rt>iah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>心裏</rb><rt>sing-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>寫</rb><rt>sia</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>胸中</rb><rt>hyüong-cong</rt></ruby>；』」<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>*ping-tsʽia*</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>*wô*</rt></ruby>，「『<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>記念</rb><rt>kyi-nyiæn</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>罪惡</rb><rt>ze-ôh</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>過犯</rb><rt>ko-væn</rt></ruby>。』」 <sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>罪惡</rb><rt>Ze-ôh</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>過犯</rb><rt>ko-væn</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>*yi-kying*</rt><rb>赦掉</rb><rt>sô-diao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好甮</rb><rt>hao-vong</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>獻</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>贖罪個</rb><rt>joh-ze-go</rt><rb>祭物</rb><rt>*tsi-veh*</rt></ruby>。

<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>KA-NI</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>靠着</rb><rt>kʽao-djôh</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>耶穌</u>個</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-go</rt><rb>血</rb><rt>hyüih</rt><rb>可以</rb><rt>kʽo-yi</rt><rb>放膽</rb><rt>fông-tæn</rt><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>聖善</rb><rt>sing-jün</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>*di-fông*</rt></ruby>；<sup>20</sup> （<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>新開</rb><rt>sing-kʽæ</rt><rb>箇埭</rb><rt>keh-da</rt><rb>簇斬新個</rb><rt>tsʽoh-tsæn-sing-go</rt><rb>活路</rb><rt>weh-lu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>箇道</rb><rt>keh-dao</rt><rb>幔帳</rb><rt>mun-tsiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>肉身</rb><rt>nyüoh-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>*ka*</rt><rb>過來個</rb><rt>*ko-læ-go*</rt></ruby>。）<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>**yiu**</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>祭司</rb><rt>Tsi-s</rt><rb>治</rb><rt>djü</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>一家</rb><rt>ih-kô</rt></ruby>，——<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>*sô-yi*</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>血</rb><rt>*hyüih*</rt><rb>扎</rb><rt>tsah</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>心裏</rb><rt>sing-li</rt><rb>棄掉</rb><rt>kyʽi-diao</rt><rb>良心</rb><rt>liang-sing</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>責罰個</rb><rt>*tsah-vah-go*</rt><rb>惡</rb><rt>ôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>清</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>滰乾淨</rb><rt>gyiang-*ken-zing*</rt><rb>肌身</rb><rt>kyi-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>誠實個</rb><rt>dzing-jih-go</rt><rb>心腸</rb><rt>sing-dziang</rt><rb>篤實個</rb><rt>toh-jih-go</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>走攏</rb><rt>tseo-long</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>*Jing-ming-go*</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>*u-sen*</rt></ruby>。

<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>想望個</rb><rt>siang-vông-go</rt><rb>福氣</rb><rt>*foh-kyʽi*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>立定</rb><rt>lih-ding</rt><rb>主意</rb><rt>cü-i</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>招認</rb><rt>tsiao-jing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>搖動</rb><rt>yiao-dong</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>應許</rb><rt>ing-hyü</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>*ah-lah*</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>Cü-kwu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>信實個</rb><rt>sing-jih-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>Ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>顧着</rb><rt>kwu-djôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>大家</rb><rt>*dô-kô*</rt><rb>勉勵</rb><rt>min-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>存</rb><rt>dzeng</rt><rb>仁愛</rb><rt>jing-æ</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>行善</rb><rt>ʽang-jün</rt></ruby>。<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>教會裏</rb><rt>*Kyiao-we-li*</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>聚攏</rb><rt>jü-long</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>有得個</rb><rt>yiu-teh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>相勸</rb><rt>*siang*-kyʽün</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt><rb>近了</rb><rt>gying-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>愈加</rb><rt>yü-kô</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>*z-ka*</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。

<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>識得</rb><rt>sih-teh</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt><rb>之後</rb><rt>ts-ʽeo</rt><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>故意</rb><rt>kwu-i</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>犯罪</rb><rt>væn-ze</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>斷</rb><rt>tön</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>贖罪個</rb><rt>joh-ze-go</rt><rb>祭物</rb><rt>tsi-veh</rt></ruby>；<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt><rb>刮刮抖</rb><rt>gwah-gwah-teo</rt><rb>等待</rb><rt>teng-dæ</rt><rb>審判</rb><rt>sing-pʽun</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>將來</rb><rt>tsiang-læ</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>喫沒</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-meh</rt><rb>仇敵</rb><rt>dziu-dih</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>*ziang*</rt><rb>猛火</rb><rt>*mang*-ho</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>*ka*</rt><rb>𢛟惶</rb><rt>ô-wông</rt></ruby>。<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>Væn-pah</rt><rb>棄掉</rb><rt>kyʽi-diao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>兩三個</rb><rt>liang-sæn-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>對證</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必</rb><rt>*pih*</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>*iao*</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>哀憐</rb><rt>æ-lin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>*gyi*</rt></ruby>。<sup>29</sup> <ruby><rb>何況</rb><rt>ʽo-hwông</rt><rb>踐踏</rb><rt>dzin-dah</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>尊起</rb><rt>tseng-kyʽi</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>立約個</rb><rt>*lih*-iah-go</rt><rb>血</rb><rt>hyüih</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>潔淨</rb><rt>kyih-zing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>糟蹋</rb><rt>tsao-tʽah</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>恩惠個</rb><rt>eng-we-go</rt><rb>聖靈</rb><rt>*Sing*-Ling</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>忖</rb><rt>tsʽeng</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>*nying*</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>更加</rb><rt>keng-kô</rt><rb>重個</rb><rt>djong-go</rt><rb>刑罰</rb><rt>ying-vah</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>誰</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>*ka*</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，『<ruby><rb>伸冤</rb><rt>Sing-ün</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>還報</rb><rt>wæn-pao</rt></ruby>。』」 <ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>CÜ</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>審判</rb><rt>sing-pʽun</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>。」 <sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>落</rb><rt>Lôh</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>活</rb><rt>weh</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>手裏</rb><rt>siu-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>真正</rb><rt>tsing-tsing</rt><rb>可</rb><rt>kʽo</rt><rb>怕</rb><rt>pʽô</rt></ruby>。

<sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>忖到</rb><rt>tsʽeng-tao</rt><rb>前頭個</rb><rt>zin-deo-go</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>光明</rb><rt>kwông-ming</rt><rb>之後</rb><rt>ts-ʽeo</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>忍耐</rb><rt>jing-næ</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>難頭</rb><rt>næn-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>正像</rb><rt>*tsing-ziang*</rt><rb>比武</rb><rt>pi-vu</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>*ka*</rt></ruby>。<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>非但</rb><rt>fi-dæn</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>「<ruby><rb>衆人個</rb><rt>cong-nying-go</rt><rb>眼前</rb><rt>ngæn-zin</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>𧮙罵</rb><rt>zôh-mô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>困苦</rb><rt>kwʽeng-kwʽu</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>別人</rb><rt>bih-nying</rt><rb>遭着</rb><rt>tsao-djôh</rt><rb>箇株介</rb><rt>keh-cü-ka</rt><rb>難頭</rb><rt>*næn-deo*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>有份</rb><rt>yiu-veng</rt></ruby>。<sup>34</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>體諒</rb><rt>tʽi-liang</rt><rb>哀憐</rb><rt>æ-lin</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>鏈條</rb><rt>lin-diao</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>人家</rb><rt>*nying-kô*</rt><rb>奪</rb><rt>deh</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>家計</rb><rt>kô-kyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>歡歡喜喜</rb><rt>hwun-hwun-hyi-hyi</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>天上</rb><rt>tʽin-zông</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>更加</rb><rt>keng-kô</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>長</rb><rt>dziang</rt><rb>來東個</rb><rt>læ-tong-go</rt><rb>家計</rb><rt>kô-kyi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] *ʽÔh* *wô*, zi hyiao-teh. 或話，自曉得。（原註）</sup>

<sup>35</sup> <ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>喪掉</rb><rt>sông-diao</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>膽量</rb><rt>tæn-liang</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>總</rb><rt>tsong</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>大大</rb><rt>do-do</rt><rb>賞賜</rb><rt>sông-s</rt></ruby>。<sup>36</sup> <ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>必</rb><rt>pih</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>忍耐</rb><rt>jing-næ</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做成</rb><rt>tso-dzing</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>旨意</rb><rt>ts-i</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>應許</rb><rt>ing-hyü</rt></ruby>。<sup>37</sup> <ruby><rb>再</rb><rt>Tsæ</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>一晌晌</rb><rt>ih-zông-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇位</rb><rt>keh-we</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>Cü-kwu</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>耽擱</rb><rt>tæn-kôh</rt></ruby>。<sup>38</sup> 「<ruby><rb>正直</rb><rt>Tsing-dzih</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>活</rb><rt>weh</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>倒退個</rb><rt>tao-tʽe-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>總</rb><rt>tsong</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>歡喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。」 <sup>39</sup> <ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>Tsih-yiu</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>倒退</rb><rt>tao-tʽe</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>滅亡</rb><rt>mih-vông</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>*nying*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>*zi*</rt><rb>活靈</rb><rt>weh-ling</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。

---

### H. XI.

<ruby><rb>難間</rb><rt>NÆN-KÆN</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>想望</rb><rt>siang-vông</rt><rb>東西個</rb><rt>*tong-si*-go</rt><rb>着落</rb><rt>dziah-lôh</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>；<ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>東西個</rb><rt>tong-si-go</rt><rb>憑據</rb><rt>bing-kyü</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>We-leh</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>長老</rb><rt>tsiang-lao</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>對證</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] *ʽÔh* *wô*, kyʽüôh-jih. 或話，確實。（原註）</sup>

<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>We-leh</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>天地</rb><rt>tʽin-di</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>靠着</rb><rt>kʽao-djôh</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>號令</rb><rt>ʽao-ling</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>造成</rb><rt>zao-dzing</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>看勒見個</rb><rt>kʽen-leh-kyin-go</rt><rb>東西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>顯出東</rb><rt>hyin-cʽih-tong</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>來個</rb><rt>læ-go</rt></ruby>。

<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>We-leh</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>亞伯</rb><rt>Üô-pah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>獻</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>祭物</rb><rt>tsi-veh</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>該隱</rb><rt>Kæ-ing</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>愈加</rb><rt>yü-kô</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>靠着</rb><rt>kʽao-djôh</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>*siang-sing*</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞伯</rb><rt>*Üô-pah*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>對證</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>正直個</rb><rt>tsing-dzih-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>對證</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>禮物</rb><rt>li-veh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>sô-yi</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>雖即</rb><rt>se-tsih</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>仍舊</rb><rt>dzing-gyiu</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>。

<sup>\[1\] *ʽÔh* *wô*, nying-kô kông-tao gyi-go z-ken. 或話，人家講到其個事幹。（原註）</sup>

<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>We-leh</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>以諾</rb><rt>Yi-nôh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>收勒去</rb><rt>siu-leh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>；「<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>收勒去</rb><rt>siu-leh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>sô-yi</rt><rb>終於</rb><rt>*cong-ü*</rt><rb>尋</rb><rt>zing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>弗着</rb><rt>feh-djôh</rt></ruby>。」 <ruby><rb>弗曾</rb><rt>feh-zing</rt><rb>收去</rb><rt>siu-kyʽi</rt><rb>之前</rb><rt>ts-zin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>對證</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>歡喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>M̆-teh</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>*Gyi*</rt><rb>歡喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>走攏</rb><rt>tseo-long</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>來個</rb><rt>læ-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>必</rb><rt>pih</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>*Gyi*</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>有個</rb><rt>yiu-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>賞賜</rb><rt>sông-s</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>虔心</rb><rt>gyin-sing</rt><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。

<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>We-leh</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>挪亞</rb><rt>Nô-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>默示</rb><rt>meh-z</rt><rb>指點</rb><rt>ts-tin</rt><rb>弗曾</rb><rt>feh-zing</rt><rb>看見個</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>心裏</rb><rt>sing-li</rt><rb>怕懼</rb><rt>pʽô-gyü</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>備辦</rb><rt>be-bæn</rt><rb>一隻</rb><rt>ih-tsah</rt><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>合家</rb><rt>*ʽeh-kô*</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>方船</rb><rt>fông-jün</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>sô-yi</rt><rb>定</rb><rt>ding</rt><rb>世間上個</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông-go</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>接着</rb><rt>tsih-djôh</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>來個</rb><rt>læ-go</rt><rb>公義</rb><rt>kong-yi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>產業</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] *ʽÔh* *wô*, dzeng tseng-kying pʽô-gyü-go sing; *ʽôh*, siao-sing kwu-djôh. 或話，存尊敬怕懼個心；或，小心顧着。（原註）</sup>

<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>We-leh</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>召來</rb><rt>dziao-læ</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>奉旨</rb><rt>vong-ts</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>將來</rb><rt>tsiang-læ</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>產業</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>*z-ʽeo*</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>何裏</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>We-leh</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>耽擱</rb><rt>tæn-kôh</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>應許個</rb><rt>ing-hyü-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>正像</rb><rt>tsing-ziang</rt><rb>別人個</rb><rt>bih-nying-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>*di-fông*</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以撒</rb><rt>Yi-sah</rt></ruby></u> <u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-kôh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>帳篷</rb><rt>tsiang-bong</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt></ruby>。（<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>*ziu-z*</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>*teng*</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>*gyi*</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>接着</rb><rt>tsih-djôh</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>應許</rb><rt>ing-hyü</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>。）<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>等待</rb><rt>teng-dæ</rt><rb>箇座</rb><rt>keh-zo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>根基</rb><rt>keng-kyi</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>建立</rb><rt>kyin-lih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>創造個</rb><rt>tsʽông-zao-go</rt></ruby>。

<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>We-leh</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>撒拉</rb><rt>Sah-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>過頭</rb><rt>ko-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>成孕</rb><rt>dzing-yüing</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>*ng-ts*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>應許</rb><rt>ing-hyü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>*gyi*</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>Cü-kwu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>信實個</rb><rt>sing-jih-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>Keh-lah</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>血氣</rb><rt>hyüih-kyʽi</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>枯燥個</rb><rt>kwʽu-sao-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>生產</rb><rt>sang-tsʽæn</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>天裏個</rb><rt>tʽin-li-go</rt><rb>星宿</rb><rt>sing-siu</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>海塗個</rb><rt>hæ-du-go</rt><rb>沙泥</rb><rt>sô-nyi</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>數弗遍</rb><rt>su-feh-pin</rt></ruby>。

<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>存</rb><rt>dzeng</rt><rb>相信個</rb><rt>siang-sing-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>死了</rb><rt>si-de</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>弗曾</rb><rt>feh-zing</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>應許</rb><rt>ing-hyü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>單只</rb><rt>tæn-tsih</rt><rb>遠遠</rb><rt>yün-yün</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>篤實</rb><rt>toh-jih</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>*gyi*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一心</rb><rt>ih-sing</rt><rb>企慕</rb><rt>kyʽi-mo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>世間上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>外來人</rb><rt>nga-læ-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>出門人</rb><rt>cʽih-meng-nying</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>Ka</rt><rb>講個</rb><rt>kông-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>明明</rb><rt>ming-ming</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>尋</rb><rt>zing</rt><rb>本國</rb><rt>peng-koh</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <sup>17, 18</sup> <ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>We-leh</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>試煉</rb><rt>s-lin</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>獻上</rb><rt>hyin-zông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以撒</rb><rt>Yi-sah</rt></ruby></u>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>接受</rb><rt>tsih-ziu</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>應許</rb><rt>ing-hyü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>*Jing-ming*</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>*yi*</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>Dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以撒</rb><rt>Yi-sah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>來個</rb><rt>læ-go</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>後代</rb><rt>ʽeo-dæ</rt></ruby>，」<ruby><rb>難間</rb><rt>*næn-kæn*</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>*wa*</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>獻上</rb><rt>hyin-zông</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>獨養</rb><rt>doh-yiang</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>*ng-ts*</rt></ruby>。<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>其自</rb><rt>*Gyi-zi*</rt><rb>忖</rb><rt>tsʽeng</rt><rb>停當</rb><rt>ding-tông</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>*gyi*</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>死個</rb><rt>si-go</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>活轉來</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>做來</rb><rt>tso-læ</rt><rb>正像</rb><rt>tsing-ziang</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>死個</rb><rt>si-go</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>得到手</rb><rt>teh-tao-siu</rt></ruby>。

<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>We-leh</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-kôh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>臨死</rb><rt>ling-si</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>祝福</rb><rt>coh-foh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>兩個</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt></ruby>「<ruby><rb>擋勒</rb><rt>tông-leh</rt><rb>哀杖</rb><rt>æ-dziang</rt><rb>拜拜</rb><rt>pa-pa</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>*Jing-ming*</rt></ruby>。」

<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>基督</u>個</rb><rt>Kyi-toh-go</rt><rb>𧮙罵</rb><rt>zôh-mô</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>一切個</rb><rt>*ih-tsʽih-go*</rt><rb>財物</rb><rt>dzæ-veh</rt><rb>更加</rb><rt>keng-kô</rt><rb>寶貝</rb><rt>pao-pe</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>仰望</rb><rt>nyiang-vông</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>後來個</rb><rt>*ʽeo-læ-go*</rt><rb>賞</rb><rt>sông</rt></ruby>。<sup>27</sup> <sup>29</sup> <ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>We-leh</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>*Yi-seh-lih*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>*nying*</rt><rb>渡過</rb><rt>du-ko</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>紅</u>海</rb><rt>ʽOng-hæ</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>*tseo*</rt><rb>燥地</rb><rt>*sao-di*</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>想</rb><rt>siang</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>走過去</rb><rt>*tseo-ko-kyʽi*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>浸煞了</rb><rt>tsing-sah-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>We-leh</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶利哥</rb><rt>Yiæ-li-ko</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>圍轉</rb><rt>we-cün</rt><rb>七</rb><rt>tsʽih</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>跌倒了</rb><rt>tih-tao-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>We-leh</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>妓女</rb><rt>gyi-nyü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>喇合</rb><rt>Lah-ʽeh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>和和氣氣</rb><rt>ʽo-ʽo-kyʽi-kyʽi</rt><rb>收留</rb><rt>siu-liu</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>探子</rb><rt>tʽen-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>依順</rb><rt>i-jing</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>滅亡</rb><rt>mih-vông</rt></ruby>。

<sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>何必</rb><rt>ʽo-pih</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>講到</rb><rt>kông-tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>其田</rb><rt>Gyi-din</rt></ruby></u>，<u><ruby><rb>巴勒</rb><rt>Pô-leh</rt></ruby></u>，<u><ruby><rb>參孫</rb><rt>Seng-seng</rt></ruby></u>，<u><ruby><rb>耶弗大</rb><rt>Yiæ-feh-da</rt></ruby></u>，<u><ruby><rb>大闢</rb><rt>Da-bih</rt></ruby></u>，<u><ruby><rb>撒母耳</rb><rt>Sah-meo-r</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>衆</rb><rt>*cong*</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>來弗及</rb><rt>læ-feh-gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>靠着</rb><rt>kʽao-djôh</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>制服</rb><rt>cü-voh</rt><rb>敵國</rb><rt>*dih*-koh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做成</rb><rt>tso-dzing</rt><rb>公義</rb><rt>kong-yi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>應許</rb><rt>ing-hyü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>鉗住</rb><rt>gyin-djü</rt><rb>獅子個</rb><rt>s-ts-go</rt><rb>口</rb><rt>kʽeo</rt></ruby>，<sup>34</sup> <ruby><rb>撲滅</rb><rt>pʽoh-mih</rt><rb>火個</rb><rt>ho-go</rt><rb>勢道</rb><rt>shü-dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>避過</rb><rt>bi-ko</rt><rb>刀口</rb><rt>tao-kʽeo</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>懦弱</rb><rt>no-ziah</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>變</rb><rt>pin</rt><rb>剛強</rb><rt>kông-gyiang</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>打仗</rb><rt>tang-tsiang</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>英雄</rb><rt>ing-yüong</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>破掉</rb><rt>pʽo-diao</rt><rb>外國人個</rb><rt>nga-koh-nying-go</rt><rb>陣勢</rb><rt>dzing-shü</rt></ruby>。<sup>35</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>其自個</rb><rt>gyi-zi-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>死仔</rb><rt>si-ts</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>活</rb><rt>weh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>Yia</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>慘酷個</rb><rt>tsʽæn-kʽoh-go</rt><rb>刑罰</rb><rt>ying-vah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>免掉</rb><rt>min-diao</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>想</rb><rt>siang</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>更</rb><rt>keng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>再活個</rb><rt>tsæ-weh-go</rt><rb>福氣</rb><rt>*foh-kyʽi*</rt></ruby>。<sup>36</sup> <ruby><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>有得個</rb><rt>yiu-teh-go</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>糟蹋</rb><rt>tsao-tʽah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>敲打</rb><rt>kʽao-tang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>鏈條</rb><rt>lin-diao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>牢監</rb><rt>lao-kæn</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>試煉</rb><rt>s-lin</rt></ruby>；<sup>37</sup> <ruby><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>石頭</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt><rb>揞</rb><rt>kʽang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>鋸</rb><rt>ken</rt><rb>解</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>引誘</rb><rt>ying-yiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>刀口</rb><rt>tao-kʽeo</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>穿</rb><rt>cʽün</rt><rb>湖羊</rb><rt>wu-yiang</rt><rb>山羊</rb><rt>sæn-yiang</rt><rb>皮</rb><rt>bi</rt><rb>走來走去</rb><rt>tseo-læ-tseo-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>嘸喫用</rb><rt>m-kyʽüoh-yüong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>困苦</rb><rt>kwʽeng-kwʽu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>傷害</rb><rt>sông-ʽæ</rt></ruby>；<sup>38</sup> <ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>曠野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>山嶺</rb><rt>sæn-ling</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>石洞</rb><rt>zah-dong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>地穴</rb><rt>di-yüih</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>東奔西走</rb><rt>tong-peng-si-tseo</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>然而</rb><rt>*jün-r*</rt><rb>世間上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>箇株介</rb><rt>keh-cü-ka</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>相宜個</rb><rt>siang-nyi-go</rt></ruby>。

<sup>39</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>雖即</rb><rt>se-tsih</rt><rb>靠着</rb><rt>kʽao-djôh</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>對證</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只是</rb><rt>*tsih-z*</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>應許</rb><rt>ing-hyü</rt></ruby>。<sup>40</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>預先</rb><rt>yü-sin</rt><rb>備辦</rb><rt>be-bæn</rt><rb>一樣</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt><rb>更</rb><rt>keng</rt><rb>好個</rb><rt>hao-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>同隊</rb><rt>dong-de</rt><rb>來東</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>全備</rb><rt>djün-be</rt></ruby>。

### H. XII.

<ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>Sô-yi</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>既然</rb><rt>kyi-jün</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>見證</rb><rt>kyin-tsing</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>*ziang*</rt><rb>雲</rb><rt>yüing</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>*ka*</rt><rb>團圈</rb><rt>dön-kyʽün</rt><rb>圍住</rb><rt>we-djü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>卸掉</rb><rt>sia-diao</rt><rb>一切個</rb><rt>ih-tsʽih-go</rt><rb>重擔</rb><rt>djong-tæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>容易</rb><rt>yüong-yi</rt><rb>纏擾</rb><rt>dzin-ziao</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>罪孽</rb><rt>ze-nyih</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>指引</rb><rt>ts-ying</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>路程</rb><rt>lu-dzing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>耐心</rb><rt>næ-sing</rt><rb>奔上去</rb><rt>peng-zông-kyʽi</rt></ruby>；<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>該</rb><rt>*kæ*</rt><rb>時常</rb><rt>*z-djông*</rt><rb>仰望</rb><rt>nyiang-vông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>*ziu-z*</rt><rb>箇位</rb><rt>keh-we</rt><rb>設立</rb><rt>shih-lih</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>完全</rb><rt>wun-djün</rt><rb>阿拉個</rb><rt>*ah-lah-go*</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>Cü-kwu</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>指點</rb><rt>ts-tin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>快樂</rb><rt>kwʽa-lôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>忍耐</rb><rt>jing-næ</rt><rb>十字架個</rb><rt>jih-z-kô-go</rt><rb>苦頭</rb><rt>*kwʽu-deo*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>顧</rb><rt>kwu</rt><rb>倒霉</rb><rt>tao-me</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>現在</rb><rt>*yin-dzæ*</rt><rb>坐</rb><rt>zo</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>座位個</rb><rt>zo-we-go</rt><rb>順手邊</rb><rt>jing-siu-pin</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>忍耐</rb><rt>jing-næ</rt><rb>罪人</rb><rt>ze-nying</rt><rb>箇株介</rb><rt>keh-cü-ka</rt><rb>橫纏</rb><rt>w̆ang-dzin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>思忖</rb><rt>s-tsʽeng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>省勒</rb><rt>sæn-leh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>疲倦</rb><rt>bi-gyün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>心裏</rb><rt>sing-li</rt><rb>脫力</rb><rt>tʽeh-lih</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] Shih-lih: *ʽôh* *wô*, ling-deo; *ʽôh*, tso Keng-nyün, Sd. 3. 15; *ʽôh*, tso Kyüing-wông, Sd. 5. 31. H. 2. 10. 設立：或話，零頭；或，做根源，Sd. 3. 15；或，做君王，Sd. 5. 31. H. 2. 10。（原註）</sup>

<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>抵擋</rb><rt>ti-tông</rt><rb>罪惡</rb><rt>ze-ôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>爭</rb><rt>tsang</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>血出</rb><rt>hyüih-cʽih</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>忘記</rb><rt>mông-kyi</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>勸</rb><rt>kyʽün</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>勸</rb><rt>kyʽün</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>*ziu-z*</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>*wô*</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>責備</rb><rt>tsah-be</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>輕易</rb><rt>kyʽing-yi</rt><rb>忽略</rb><rt>hweh-liah</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>批評</rb><rt>pʽi-bing</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>脫力</rb><rt>tʽeh-lih</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>愛惜個</rb><rt>æ-sih-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>總</rb><rt>*tsong*</rt><rb>責備</rb><rt>tsah-be</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>收留個</rb><rt>siu-liu-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>總</rb><rt>tsong</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。」 <sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>耐心</rb><rt>næ-sing</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>責備</rb><rt>tsah-be</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>待</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>何裏</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>責備</rb><rt>tsah-be</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>難間</rb><rt>Næn-kæn</rt><rb>衆</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>*ng-ts*</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>責備</rb><rt>tsah-be</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>責備</rb><rt>tsah-be</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是個</rb><rt>z-go</rt><rb>野種</rb><rt>yia-cong</rt></ruby>。

<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>再話</rb><rt>Tsæ-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>肉身個</rb><rt>nyüoh-sing-go</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt><rb>責備</rb><rt>tsah-be</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尚且</rb><rt>jông-tsʽia</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>敬重</rb><rt>kying-djong</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>何況</rb><rt>ʽo-hwông</rt><rb>靈性個</rb><rt>ling-sing-go</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>更加</rb><rt>keng-kô</rt><rb>服</rb><rt>voh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>活命</rb><rt>weh-ming</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>肉身個</rb><rt>*nyüoh-sing-go*</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>*ah-tia*</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>意思</rb><rt>i-s</rt><rb>暫時</rb><rt>dzæn-z</rt><rb>責備</rb><rt>tsah-be</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>箇位</rb><rt>keh-we</rt><rb>靈性個</rb><rt>*ling-sing-go*</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>*Ah-tia*</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>阿拉個</rb><rt>*ah-lah-go*</rt><rb>益處</rb><rt>ih-cʽü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>聖善</rb><rt>sing-jün</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>至於</rb><rt>Ts-ü</rt><rb>凡百個</rb><rt>væn-pah-go</rt><rb>責備</rb><rt>tsah-be</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>責備</rb><rt>tsah-be</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>好像</rb><rt>hao-ziang</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>快樂</rb><rt>kwʽa-lôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt><rb>憂悶</rb><rt>iu-meng</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>結</rb><rt>kyih</rt><rb>和平</rb><rt>ʽo-bing</rt><rb>公義個</rb><rt>kong-yi-go</rt><rb>果子</rb><rt>ko-ts</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>練練</rb><rt>lin-lin</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>。

<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>Sô-yi</rt></ruby>「<ruby><rb>嘸力個</rb><rt>m-lih-go</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>軟弱個</rb><rt>nyün-ziah-go</rt><rb>腳䯊頭</rb><rt>kyiah-kʽo-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>敦</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>起來</rb><rt>kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>。」 <sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>鋪直</rb><rt>pʽu-dzih</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>*gyi*</rt><rb>路</rb><rt>lu</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾拉個</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>腳</rb><rt>kyiah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>*hao*</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>*tseo*</rt></ruby>」 ，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>拐腳個</rb><rt>kwa-kyiah-go</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>走錯</rb><rt>tseo-tsʽo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>反轉</rb><rt>fæn-cün</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>醫好</rb><rt>i-hao</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>要想</rb><rt>iao-siang</rt><rb>接着</rb><rt>tsih-djôh</rt><rb>長子個</rb><rt>*tsiang-ts-go*</rt><rb>祝福</rb><rt>coh-foh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>棄掉</rb><rt>kyʽi-diao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>雖即</rb><rt>se-tsih</rt><rb>眼淚</rb><rt>ngæn-li</rt><rb>浡出</rb><rt>beh-cʽih</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt><rb>挽回之</rb><rt>w̆æn-we-ts</rt><rb>處</rb><rt>cʽü</rt></ruby><sup> ### H. XIII.

<ruby><rb>該</rb><rt>KÆ</rt><rb>時常</rb><rt>z-djông</rt><rb>親愛</rb><rt>tsʽing-æ</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>Iao</rt><rb>殷勤</rb><rt>ing-gying</rt><rb>接待</rb><rt>tsih-dæ</rt><rb>出門人</rb><rt>cʽih-meng-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>忘記</rb><rt>mông-kyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>前頭</rb><rt>*zin-deo*</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>做法</rb><rt>tso-fah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗知弗覺</rb><rt>feh-cü-feh-kôh</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>接待</rb><rt>tsih-dæ</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>Yüong</rt><rb>記念</rb><rt>kyi-nyiæn</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>鏈條</rb><rt>lin-diao</rt><rb>鎖住間個</rb><rt>so-djü-kæn-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>爾自</rb><rt>ng-zi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>*teng*</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>*gyi*</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>鎖住</rb><rt>so-djü</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>記念</rb><rt>*kyi-nyiæn*</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>受難個</rb><rt>ziu-næn-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>爾自</rb><rt>ng-zi</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>受難個</rb><rt>*ziu-næn-go*</rt><rb>肌身</rb><rt>kyi-sing</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>婚姻</rb><rt>Hweng-ing</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>衆人</rb><rt>cong-*nying*</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>看重</rb><rt>kʽen-djong</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>眠牀</rb><rt>min-zông</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>*kæ*</rt><rb>嘸瑕點個</rb><rt>m-yüô-tin-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>淫亂</rb><rt>ying-lön</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>姦淫個</rb><rt>kæn-ying-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>判斷</rb><rt>pʽun-tön</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>做人</rb><rt>Tso-nying</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>存</rb><rt>dzeng</rt><rb>貪財個</rb><rt>tʽen-dzæ-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>有個</rb><rt>yiu-go</rt><rb>東西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>知足</rb><rt>cü-tsoh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>離開</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>棄掉</rb><rt>kyʽi-diao</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>。」 <sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>sô-yi</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>放膽</rb><rt>fông-tæn</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>CÜ</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>幫手</rb><rt>pông-siu</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>怕</rb><rt>pʽô</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>待</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>。」 

<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>以前</rb><rt>yi-zin</rt><rb>引導</rb><rt>ying-dao</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>傳</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>記念</rb><rt>kyi-nyiæn</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>仔細</rb><rt>ts-si</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>行意動靜個</rb><rt>ʽang-i-dong-zing-go</rt><rb>歸根</rb><rt>kwe-keng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>學</rb><rt>ʽôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>相信個</rb><rt>siang-sing-go</rt><rb>樣</rb><rt>yiang</rt></ruby>。

<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>前頭</rb><rt>Zin-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>難間</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>世世代代</rb><rt>shü-shü-dæ-dæ</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u> <u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>永</rb><rt>üong</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>更改</rb><rt>keng-kæ</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>M-nao</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>有夥</rb><rt>yiu-ho</rt><rb>號數</rb><rt>ʽao-su</rt><rb>邪教</rb><rt>zia-kyiao</rt><rb>搖動</rb><rt>yiao-dong</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>心裏</rb><rt>sing-li</rt><rb>靠着</rb><rt>kʽao-djôh</rt><rb>恩惠</rb><rt>eng-we</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>堅固</rb><rt>kyin-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>好個</rb><rt>hao-go</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-*kʽo*</rt><rb>靠着</rb><rt>kʽao-djôh</rt><rb>喫食</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-zih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>留心</rb><rt>liu-sing</rt><rb>喫食</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-zih</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>無益個</rb><rt>vu-ih-go</rt></ruby>。

<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>祭壇</rb><rt>tsi-dæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>禮物</rb><rt>*li-veh*</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>帳篷裏</rb><rt>tsiang-bong-li</rt><rb>奉侍個</rb><rt>vong-z-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>牲口</rb><rt>sang-kʽeo</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>血</rb><rt>hyüih</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>祭司頭</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>帶進</rb><rt>ta-tsing</rt><rb>聖善</rb><rt>sing-jün</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>贖</rb><rt>joh</rt><rb>人個</rb><rt>*nying-go*</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>身體</rb><rt>sing-tʽi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>營寨外</rb><rt>ying-za-nga</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>火</rb><rt>*ho*</rt><rb>𤈦掉</rb><rt>me-diao</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>sô-yi</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>Zi-go</rt><rb>血</rb><rt>hyüih</rt><rb>尊起</rb><rt>tseng-kyʽi</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>城門外</rb><rt>dzing-meng-nga</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>苦</rb><rt>kwʽu</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>*yia*</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt><rb>營寨外</rb><rt>ying-za-nga</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>情願</rb><rt>*dzing-nyün*</rt><rb>擔當</rb><rt>tæn-tông</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>𧮙罵</rb><rt>zôh-mô</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>蕩頭</rb><rt>dông-deo</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>長</rb><rt>dziang</rt><rb>來東個</rb><rt>læ-tong-go</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>單</rb><rt>tæn</rt><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt><rb>箇座</rb><rt>keh-zo</rt><rb>將來個</rb><rt>tsiang-læ-go</rt></ruby>。

<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>Sô-yi</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>靠着</rb><rt>kʽao-djôh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>讚美個</rb><rt>tsæn-me-go</rt><rb>祭物</rb><rt>tsi-veh</rt><rb>時刻</rb><rt>z-kʽeh</rt><rb>獻上</rb><rt>hyin-zông</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>*ah-lah*</rt><rb>口裏</rb><rt>kʽeo-li</rt><rb>感謝</rb><rt>ken-zia</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>名頭</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>果子</rb><rt>ko-ts</rt></ruby>。<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>Ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>行</rb><rt>ʽang</rt><rb>好事</rb><rt>hao-z</rt><rb>周濟</rb><rt>tsiu-tsi</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>*nying-kô*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>忘記</rb><rt>mông-kyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇種介</rb><rt>keh-cong-ka</rt><rb>祭物</rb><rt>tsi-veh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>歡喜個</rb><rt>hwun-hyi-go</rt></ruby>。

<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>引導</rb><rt>ying-dao</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>警醒</rb><rt>kying-sing</rt><rb>顧着</rb><rt>kwu-djôh</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>靈魂</rb><rt>ling-weng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>正像</rb><rt>tsing-ziang</rt><rb>將來</rb><rt>tsiang-læ</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>交代</rb><rt>kao-dæ</rt><rb>賬目</rb><rt>tsiang-moh</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>依順</rb><rt>i-jing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>投伏</rb><rt>deo-voh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>歡歡喜喜</rb><rt>hwun-hwun-hyi-hyi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好甮</rb><rt>hao-vong</rt><rb>嗐聲嘆氣</rb><rt>ʽæ-sing-tʽæn-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>*ziah*</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>*feh-z*</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>*ka*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>於</rb><rt>ü</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>無益</rb><rt>vu-ih</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] *ʽÔh* *wô*, ng-lah li-hyiang tso we-deo-we-nao go. 或話，爾拉裏向做爲頭爲腦個。（原註）</sup>

<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>Yüong</rt><rb>替</rb><rt>tʽi</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>禱告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>忖忖</rb><rt>tsʽeng-tsʽeng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>存好個</rb><rt>dzeng-hao-go</rt><rb>良心</rb><rt>liang-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>巴弗能夠</rb><rt>pô-feh-neng-keo</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>循規蹈矩</rb><rt>jing-kwe-dao-kyü</rt><rb>做人</rb><rt>tso-nying</rt></ruby>。<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>愈加</rb><rt>yü-kô</rt><rb>血心</rb><rt>hyüih-sing</rt><rb>煩</rb><rt>væn</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>禱告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>速速</rb><rt>soh-soh</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>爾拉個</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。

<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>難間</rb><rt>Næn-kæn</rt><rb>箇位</rb><rt>keh-we</rt><rb>平安個</rb><rt>bing-en-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>箇位</rb><rt>keh-we</rt><rb>看羊個</rb><rt>*kʽen*-yiang-*go*</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>牧師</rb><rt>Moh-s</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>永</rb><rt>üong</rt><rb>約個</rb><rt>iah-go</rt><rb>血</rb><rt>hyüih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>死個</rb><rt>si-go</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>帶上來</rb><rt>ta-zông-læ</rt></ruby>，——<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>但願</rb><rt>dæn-nyün</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>德氣</rb><rt>*teh-kyʽi*</rt><rb>完全</rb><rt>wun-djün</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>*kyʽi*</rt><rb>行</rb><rt>ʽang</rt><rb>一切個</rb><rt>ih-tsʽih-go</rt><rb>善事</rb><rt>jün-z</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>做成</rb><rt>tso-dzing</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>旨意</rb><rt>ts-i</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>靠着</rb><rt>kʽao-djôh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u> <u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>其自</rb><rt>Gyi-zi</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>歡喜個</rb><rt>hwun-hyi-go</rt><rb>心想</rb><rt>*sing-siang*</rt><rb>存</rb><rt>dzeng</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>心裏</rb><rt>*sing*-li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但願</rb><rt>Dæn-nyün</rt><rb>榮華</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt><rb>世世代代</rb><rt>shü-shü-dæ-dæ</rt><rb>歸</rb><rt>kwe</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>亞們</rb><rt>Üô-meng</rt></ruby>。

<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>Hyüong-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>略略</rb><rt>liah-liah</rt><rb>寫</rb><rt>sia</rt><rb>兩句</rb><rt>liang-kyü</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>請</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>耐心</rb><rt>næ-sing</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>勸勉個</rb><rt>kyʽün-min-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>。<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>提摩太</rb><rt>Di-mo-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>放出</rb><rt>fông-cʽih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>速即</rb><rt>soh-tsih</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>同隊</rb><rt>dong-de</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>*læ*</rt><rb>見</rb><rt>kyin</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>。<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>煩</rb><rt>Væn</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>望望</rb><rt>mông-mông</rt><rb>衆位</rb><rt>cong-we</rt><rb>引導</rb><rt>ying-dao</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>衆</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>聖徒</rb><rt>sing-du</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以大利</rb><rt>Yi-da-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>*hyüong-di*</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>望望</rb><rt>mông-mông</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>。<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>但願</rb><rt>Dæn-nyün</rt><rb>恩惠</rb><rt>eng-we</rt><rb>臨着</rb><rt>ling-djôh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>衆人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>亞們</rb><rt>Üô-meng</rt></ruby>。
